 "old_string": "/** SpiderCaves pass（L17455）1:1：w*0.005 个巢；采样 x∈[200,w-200) y∈[(ws+rock)/2,h-230)；\n *  countTiles 门 500≤空腔<3500（重试上限 w/2，超限放弃该巢） */\nexport function runSpiderNestPass(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState, chests: ChestSink): void {\n  const count = Math.floor(st.w * 0.005);\n  const retryCap = Math.floor(st.w / 2);\n  const yMin = Math.floor((gs.worldSurface + gs.rockLevel) / 2);\n  for (let n = 0; n < count; n++) {\n    let x = rng.int(200, st.w - 201);\n    let y = rng.int(yMin, st.h - 231);\n    let c = countTiles(st, x, y);\n    let tries = 0;\n    while ((c.count >= MAX_TILE_COUNT || c.count < 500) && tries < retryCap) {\n      tries++;\n      x = rng.int(200, st.w - 201);\n      y = rng.int(yMin, st.h - 231);\n      c = countTiles(st, x, y);\n      if (c.shroom > 1) c.count = 0;\n    }\n    if (tries < retryCap) spreadSpider(st, rng, gs, chests, x, y);\n  }\n}",
 "new_string": "/** SpiderCaves pass（L17455）1:1：w*0.005 个巢；采样 x∈[200,w-200) y∈[(ws+rock)/2,h-230)；\n *  countTiles 门 500≤空腔<3500（重试上限 w/2，超限放弃该巢；重掷 y 域换 rockLevel+30） */\nexport function runSpiderNestPass(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState, chests: ChestSink): void {\n  const count = Math.floor(st.w * 0.005);\n  const retryCap = Math.floor(st.w / 2);\n  const yMin = Math.floor((gs.worldSurface + gs.rockLevel) / 2);\n  for (let n = 0; n < count; n++) {\n    let x = rng.int(200, st.w - 201);\n    let y = rng.int(yMin, st.h - 231);\n    let c = countTiles(st, x, y);\n    let tries = 0;\n    while ((c.count >= MAX_TILE_COUNT || c.count < 500) && tries < retryCap) {\n      tries++;\n      // cs:17502-17504：重掷 x 仍 [200,w-200)，y 换 [rockLayer+30, h-230)\n      x = rng.int(200, st.w - 201);\n      y = rng.int(gs.rockLevel + 30, st.h - 231);\n      c = countTiles(st, x, y);\n      if (c.shroom > 1) c.count = 0;\n    }\n    if (tries < retryCap) spreadSpider(st, rng, gs, chests, x, y);\n  }\n}"
}

Now item 23: BeeLarva timing. Change runBeehivePass to register larva positions into gs arrays, and add runBeeLarvaPass. GenState needs larvaX/larvaY arrays.

In runBeehivePass, replace the larva placement block with registration:
```ts

if (st.inBounds(lx, ly + 1)) {

}
```
But GenVars arrays have fixed size; push is fine with number[].

Then new exported function:

```ts
/** BeeLarvaInBeehives pass（cs:21018-21050）1:1：蜂巢 pass 只登记 larvaX/Y，
 *  幼虫放置（3×4 清空+底行 225+PlaceTile 231）推迟到管线尾（原版注册序 21018，
 *  位于 LongMoss(20915) 之后、SettleLiquidsPart2(21051) 之前）。 */
export function runBeeLarvaPass(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState): void {
  void rng;
  for (let n = 0; n < gs.larvaX.length; n++) {
    const x = gs.larvaX[n], y = gs.larvaY[n];
    for (let j = x - 1; j <= x + 1; j++) {
      for (let k = y - 2; k <= y + 1; k++) {
        if (!st.inBounds(j, k)) continue;
        const i = st.idx(j, k);
        if (k !== y + 1) { st.flags[i] = 0; }
        else {
          st.type[i] = HIVE; st.flags[i] = 1; st.slope[i] = 0; st.half[i] = 0;
        }
      }
    }
    // PlaceTile(231)（本仓近似：3×3 幼虫块帧）
    for (let dx = 0; dx < 3; dx++) {
      for (let dy = 0; dy < 3; dy++) {
        const tx = x - 1 + dx, ty = y - 2 + dy;
        if (!st.inBounds(tx, ty)) continue;
        const i = st.idx(tx, ty);
        st.type[i] = LARVA; st.flags[i] = 1;
        st.frameX[i] = dx * 18; st.frameY[i] = dy * 18;
      }
    }
  }
}
```
Wait: clearing k from y-2..y+1: `k != y+1 → inactive` clears rows y-2, y-1, y. The larva occupies y-2..y (3 rows) → matches the 3×3 larva frame placement after clearing. ✓ consistent with previous inline code.

GenState: add `larvaX: number[]; larvaY: number[];` and init as [].

Then WorldGen.ts: add slot. Let me now make these edits.

 "new_string": "/** Surface Chests pass(L8019):w*0.005 个地表木箱(style 0;墙 244 → 天空箱 style 12) */\nexport function runSurfaceChestsPass(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState, chests: ChestSink): void {\n  const { w } = st;\n  const count = Math.floor(w * 0.005);\n  for (let n = 0; n < count; n++) {\n    let ok = false;\n    for (let tries = 0; tries < 2000 && !ok; tries++) {\n      // cs:17244 首掷 Next(200, w-200);仅落点命中 oceanDepths(cs:17246-17254:\n      // y ≤ oceanLevel=(ws+rl)/2+40 且 x 在海滩带内)才改掷 Next(300, w-300)——\n      // 原版 WorldGen.beachDistance 是常量 380(cs:4102)\n      let x = rng.int(200, w - 201);\n      let y = rng.int(Math.floor(gs.worldSurfaceLow), Math.floor(gs.worldSurface) - 1);\n      const oceanLevel = (gs.worldSurface + gs.rockLevel) / 2 + 40;\n      while (y <= oceanLevel && (x < gs.beachDistance || x > w - gs.beachDistance)) {\n        x = rng.int(300, w - 301);\n        y = rng.int(Math.floor(gs.worldSurfaceLow), Math.floor(gs.worldSurface) - 1);\n      }\n      if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) continue;\n      const i = st.idx(x, y);\n      // cs:17260-17284 两分支：空格走墙门（2/59/244，墙 244 → 天空箱）；\n      // 落点活性格走 101×101（步 2）蓄水池采样找墙 244 空格（Next(num8) 命中即换点）\n      let sky = false;\n      let cx = x, cy = y;\n      if (!st.flags[i]) {\n        if (st.wall[i] === 2 || st.wall[i] === 59 || st.wall[i] === 244) {\n          if (st.wall[i] === 244) sky = true;\n        } else {\n          continue;   // flag3=false → 本轮作废\n        }\n      } else {\n        let num8 = 1;\n        let hit = false;\n        for (let j = cx - 50; j <= cx + 50; j += 2) {\n          for (let k = cy - 50; k <= cy + 50; k += 2) {\n            if (k >= gs.worldSurface) continue;\n            if (!st.inBounds(j, k) || st.flags[st.idx(j, k)]) continue;\n            if (st.wall[st.idx(j, k)] !== 244) continue;\n            if (rng.int(0, num8 - 1) !== 0) continue;                    // Next(num8)==0\n            sky = true;\n            num8++;\n            hit = true;\n            cx = j; cy = k;\n          }\n        }\n        if (!hit) continue;   // 蓄水池无墙 244 空格 → 本轮作废\n      }\n      const flags: ChestFlags = sky ? { sky: true } : { wooden: true };\n      if (placeBuriedChest(st, rng, gs, chests, cx, cy, { style: sky ? 12 : 0, flags })) ok = true;\n    }\n  }\n}\n\n/** UnderwaterChests 的 9×w/4200 段（cs:17381-17453）：每下标两趟 while 各 1 箱——\n *  ① y∈[1, UnderworldLayer)（内层液体重掷域 [50, UnderworldLayer)）\n *  ② y∈[worldSurface, UnderworldLayer)。主件序：单次 Next(10)==0 → 863，\n *  否则计数器 186→4404→277→187（num4 每迭代无条件 ++，命中 863 也前进）。 */\nexport function runWaterChestsPass(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState, chests: ChestSink): void {\n  const { w } = st;\n  const num5 = w / 4200;\n  const count = 9 * num5;\n  let num4 = 0;\n  for (let n = 0; n < count; n++) {\n    num4++;\n    let main: number;\n    if (rng.int(0, 9) === 0) {                                          // Next(10)\n      main = 863;\n    } else {\n      switch (num4) {\n        case 1: main = 186; break;\n        case 2: main = 4404; break;\n        case 3: main = 277; break;\n        default: main = 187; num4 = 0; break;\n      }\n    }\n    // 第一趟（cs:17424-17431）：y 首掷 [1, h-200)，重掷 [50, h-200)\n    for (let loop = 0; loop < 2; loop++) {\n      const yLo = loop === 0 ? 1 : Math.floor(gs.worldSurface);\n      let ok = false;\n      for (let tries = 0; tries < 10000 && !ok; tries++) {\n        let x = rng.int(50, w - 51);                                    // Next(50, w-50)\n        let y = rng.int(yLo, st.h - 201);                               // Next(?, h-200)\n        // cs:17425-17428：液体不足或非水 → 重掷（重掷 y 域换 [50, h-200)）\n        while (st.liquid[st.idx(x, y)] < 250 || st.liquidType[st.idx(x, y)] !== 0) {\n          x = rng.int(50, w - 51);\n          y = rng.int(50, st.h - 201);\n        }\n        // cs:17427：trySlope 门 = 落点在海滩带内（x < 380 || x > w-380）\n        if (placeBuriedChest(st, rng, gs, chests, x, y, {\n          style: 17, loot: main, flags: { water: true },\n          trySlope: x < gs.beachDistance || x > w - gs.beachDistance,\n        })) ok = true;\n      }\n    }\n  }\n}"
}

    }
  }
  if (gy >= st.h - 10) {
    if (opts.trySlope) { st.slope[st.idx(i - 1, gy)] = slopeL; st.slope[st.idx(i, gy)] = slopeR; }
    return false;
  }
  if (!solidTileHB(st, i, gy)) {
    if (opts.trySlope) { st.slope[st.idx(i - 1, gy)] = slopeL; st.slope[st.idx(i, gy)] = slopeR; }
    return false;
  }

  let tile = opts.tile ?? CHEST;
  let style = opts.style;
  const flags: ChestFlags = { ...(opts.flags ?? {}) };
  let mainId = 0;
  let mainKey: string | null = null;
  if (typeof opts.loot === 'string') {
    mainKey = opts.loot;
    mainId = vanillaIdOfKey(opts.loot) ?? 0;
    if (mainId === 0 && ITEM_BY_KEY[opts.loot] === undefined) mainKey = null; // 无效键丢弃
  } else if (opts.loot !== undefined) {
    mainId = opts.loot;
  }
  if (style === undefined) {
    if (gy > st.h - 205) {
      // 地狱箱:style 4 + 地狱武器序(cs:36158-36171)
      style = 4;
      flags.hell = true;
      if (!mainId) mainId = nextHellItem(gs);
    } else {
      style = gy >= gs.worldSurface + 25 ? 1 : 0;
      if (style === 0) flags.wooden = true;
      const tBelow = st.type[st.idx(i, gy)];
      // 冰箱门禁(cs:36126:num7 ≥ ws+25 且 ≤ h-205)
      if (!mainId && gy >= gs.worldSurface + 25 && (tBelow === SNOW || tBelow === ICE || tBelow === THIN_ICE)) {
        style = 11;
        flags.ice = true;
        mainId = [670, 724, 950, 1319, 987, 1579][rng.int(0, 5)]; // cs:36121-36125
        if (rng.int(0, 19) === 0) mainId = 997; // 1/20 提炼机
        if (rng.int(0, 49) === 0) mainId = 669; // 1/50 鱼
      }
      const ud = gs.undergroundDesert;
      if (!mainId && ud && i >= ud.x0 && i <= ud.x1 && gy >= ud.y0 && gy <= ud.y1
        && gy >= gs.worldSurface + 25 && gy <= st.h - 205) {
        tile = CHEST2;
        style = 10;
        flags.desert = true;
        const deep = gy > (ud.y0 * 3 + ud.y1 * 4) / 7;
        mainId = deep
          ? [4061, 4062, 4276][rng.int(0, 2)]
          : [4056, 4055, 4262, 4263][rng.int(0, 3)];
      }
    }
  }

  // 2×2 放置:顶行 frameY=0、frameX = style*36 + dx*18(PlaceChestDirect 语义)
  const topY = gy - 2;
  if (topY < 1) return false;
  for (let dx = 0; dx < 2; dx++) {
    for (let dy = 0; dy < 2; dy++) {
      if (!st.inBounds(i + dx, topY + dy) || st.flags[st.idx(i + dx, topY + dy)]) return false;
    }
  }
  if (!st.inBounds(i + 1, gy) || !st.flags[st.idx(i + 1, gy)]
    || !TILE_DEFS[st.type[st.idx(i + 1, gy)]]?.solid) return false;
  const fw = TILE_DEFS[tile].vanilla?.fw ?? 2;
  for (let dx = 0; dx < 2; dx++) {
    for (let dy = 0; dy < 2; dy++) {
      st.setTileSilent(i + dx, topY + dy, tile, style * fw * 18 + dx * 18, dy * 18);
    }
  }
  // 战利品:四深度分支完整表(此前仅主件+火把/药水,体感稀缺主因之一)
  const items = rollChestLoot({ rng, gs, gy, flags, mainKey, worldH: st.h }, mainId);
  chests.push({ x: i, y: topY, items });
  return true;
}

/** Buried Chests pass:L7907 洞穴段(CaveChestCount 35-40 × WorldArea/5040000)。
 *  ★地狱段拆出 runUnderworldChestsPass:本函数曾在 hellPass 之前执行,
 *  地狱箱被后续地狱地形重写 100% 抹除(实际 0 个) */
export function runBuriedChestsPass(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState, chests: ChestSink): void {
  const { w, h } = st;
  const yMin = Math.floor((gs.worldSurfaceHigh + 20 + gs.rockLevel) / 2);
  // 洞穴箱:35-40 × 面积缩放(Configuration.json CaveChestCount ScaleWith:WorldArea;
  // 小世界(4200×1200=5040000)= 1.0 → 35-40;中 ×2.29;大 ×4.0)
  const target = Math.round(rng.int(35, 40) * (w * h) / 5040000);
  let fail = 10000;
  for (let n = 0; n < target && fail > 0; ) {
    const x = rng.int(20, w - 21);
    const y = rng.int(yMin, h - 231);
    const wl = st.inBounds(x, y) ? st.wall[st.idx(x, y)] : -1;
    // cs:17109：wallDungeon[wall] || wall == 87（神庙墙 87 独立门）→ 整体重掷
    if (isDungeonWall(wl) || wl === 87 || !placeBuriedChest(st, rng, gs, chests, x, y)) {
      fail--; continue;
    }
    n++;
  }

```

````
Note: the style-17 ±30 box check happens inside the loop before flatten; those `return false` paths happen before flatten — fine.

But there's an ordering issue: vanilla does the style-17 box check and slope flatten for EVERY row k as it falls? No — re-reading vanilla: the trySlope block is inside the `for k` loop, executed at each row k before the `if (!SolidTile(i,k)) continue;` check. Wait:

```
for (int k = j; k < Main.maxTilesY - 10; k++) {
  ... shimmer/larva checks ...
  if (trySlope && Main.tile[i, k].active() && tileSolid && !solidTop) { ...box check + flatten num2/num3... }
  if (remix...) return false;
  boulder check (±2)...
  if (!SolidTile(i, k)) continue;
  ... placement ...
}
```
So the trySlope flatten happens at the FIRST active solid row encountered while falling (before knowing if it's the landing tile via SolidTile). Actually `Main.tile[i,k].active() && tileSolid && !solidTop` is basically SolidTile (minus half/slope checks). So flatten at the first solid-ish row. Then if `!SolidTile(i,k)` (e.g. half brick), continue to next row — but slopes were already flattened at this row and num2/num3 get re-assigned at the next solid row... and on final failure, restore uses the LAST num2/num3 values. That's vanilla's quirky semantics.

My implementation flattens only at the landing row (solidTileHB true). Close enough; deviation minor (vanilla may flatten at a half-brick row first). Acceptable; note in comment? I'll leave it — behaviorally the restore-on-failure is the visible part.

Now item 2①: rewrite runUnderwaterChestsPass in OceanCavesPass.ts.

 "new_string": "/** UnderwaterChests pass 的宝藏点段（cs:17348-17368）1:1：\n *  主件 NextFromList [863,186,277,187,4404]；num 自 2.0 起步进 0.1 扩窗重试\n *  （<50），采样域 [x±num, y±num/2]（上界 +1 含端），越右界回折 num/2；\n *  命中液体 ≥250 且为水 → AddBuriedChest(style17, trySlope:true)。 */\nexport function runUnderwaterChestsPass(\n  st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState, chests: ChestSink, treasures: Array<{ x: number; y: number }>,\n): void {\n  for (const t of treasures) {\n    const main = [863, 186, 277, 187, 4404][rng.int(0, 4)];        // NextFromList\n    let num = 2.0;\n    for (;;) {\n      if (num >= 50.0) break;\n      num += 0.1;\n      let x = rng.int(t.x - Math.floor(num), t.x + Math.floor(num));    // Next(x-num, x+num+1)\n      const y = rng.int(t.y - Math.floor(num / 2), t.y + Math.floor(num / 2));\n      // cs:17362：num2 越右界 → 折回 num/2（InWorld 由 placeBuriedChest 自行兜底）\n      x = x >= st.w ?  "old_string": "/** pass 76 Traps 主体（cs:8936-8984）：w*0.05 次 placeTrap + w*0.003 次 PlaceSandTrap */\nexport function runTrapsPass(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState): void {\n  bouldersPlaced.length = 0; // 模块级状态须按次生成重置，否则同种子双生成发散\n  const trapTries = Math.floor(st.w * 0.05);\n  for (let n = 0; n < trapTries; n++) {\n    for (let k = 0; k < 1150; k++) {\n      let x = rng.int(200, st.w - 201);                       // Next(200, w-200)\n      let y = rng.int(gs.worldSurface, st.h - 211);           // Next(surface, h-210)\n      // oceanDepths 回避\n      const oceanTop = (gs.worldSurface + gs.rockLevel) / 2 + 40;\n      const beachL = gs.beachLeftEnd, beachR = st.w - gs.beachRightStart;\n      while (y <= oceanTop && (x < beachL || x > beachR)) {\n        x = rng.int(200, st.w - 201);\n        y = rng.int(gs.worldSurface, st.h - 211);\n      }\n      if (st.wall[st.idx(x, y)] === 0 && placeTrap(st, rng, gs, x, y, -1)) break;\n    }\n  }\n  const sandTries = Math.floor(st.w * 0.003);\n  for (let n = 0; n < sandTries; n++) {\n    for (let k = 0; k < 20000; k++) {\n      const x = rng.int(Math.floor(st.w * 0.15), Math.floor(st.w * 0.85) - 1);\n      const y = rng.int(gs.worldSurface + 20, st.h - 211);\n      if (st.wall[st.idx(x, y)] === 187 && placeSandTrap(st, rng, x, y)) break;\n    }\n  }\n}",

 "old_string": "/** SurfaceOreAndStone pass（cs:18559）：先矿脉后石块，均带中心带回避 + 矿点登记 */\nexport function runSurfaceOreAndStonePass(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState): void {\n  const w = st.w;\n  let num = rng.int(((w * 5) / 4200) | 0, ((w * 10) / 4200) | 0);       // Next(w*5/4200, w*10/4200)\n  for (let i = 0; i < num; i++) {\n    let tries = (w / 420) | 0;\n    while (tries > 0) {\n      tries--;\n      let x = rng.int(gs.beachDistance, w - gs.beachDistance);\n      while (x >= w * 0.48 && x <= w * 0.52) x = rng.int(gs.beachDistance, w - gs.beachDistance);\n      const y = rng.int(gs.worldSurfaceLow, gs.worldSurface);           // Next((int)wsLow, (int)ws)\n      let near = false;\n      for (let j = 0; j < gs.numOrePatch; j++) {\n        if (Math.abs(x - gs.orePatchX[j]) < 200) near = true;\n      }\n      if (!near && orePatch(st, rng, gs, x, y)) {\n        if (gs.numOrePatch < 49) {                                     // maxOrePatch-1\n          gs.orePatchX[gs.numOrePatch] = x;\n          gs.numOrePatch++;\n        }\n        break;\n      }\n    }\n  }\n  num = rng.int(1, ((w * 7) / 4200) | 0);                               // Next(1, w*7/4200)\n  for (let k = 0; k < num; k++) {\n    let tries = (w / 420) | 0;\n    while (tries > 0) {\n      tries--;\n      let x = rng.int(gs.beachDistance, w - gs.beachDistance);\n      while (x >= w * 0.47 && x <= w * 0.53) x = rng.int(gs.beachDistance, w - gs.beachDistance);\n      const y = rng.int(gs.worldSurfaceLow, gs.worldSurface);\n      let near = false;\n      for (let l = 0; l < gs.numOrePatch; l++) {\n        if (Math.abs(x - gs.orePatchX[l]) < 100) near = true;\n      }\n      if (!near && stonePatch(st, rng, gs, x, y)) break;\n    }\n  }\n}",
 "new_string": "/** SurfaceOreAndStone pass（cs:18559）：先矿脉后石块，均带中心带回避 + 矿点登记。\n *  rng.int 闭区间 ≡ Next(a, b+1)——所有上界传原版第二参 -1。 */\nexport function runSurfaceOreAndStonePass(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState): void {\n  const w = st.w;\n  let num = rng.int(((w * 5) / 4200) | 0, ((w * 10) / 4200) | 0 - 1);   // Next(w*5/4200, w*10/4200)\n  for (let i = 0; i < num; i++) {\n    let tries = (w / 420) | 0;\n    while (tries > 0) {\n      tries--;\n      let x = rng.int(gs.beachDistance, w - gs.beachDistance - 1);     // Next(bd, w-bd)\n      while (x >= w * 0.48 && x <= w * 0.52) x = rng.int(gs.beachDistance, w - gs.beachDistance - 1);\n      const y = rng.int(gs.worldSurfaceLow, gs.worldSurface - 1);      // Next((int)wsLow, (int)ws)\n      let near = false;\n      for (let j = 0; j < gs.numOrePatch; j++) {\n        if (Math.abs(x - gs.orePatchX[j]) < 200) near = true;\n      }\n      if (!near && orePatch(st, rng, gs, x, y)) {\n        if (gs.numOrePatch < 49) {                                     // maxOrePatch-1\n          gs.orePatchX[gs.numOrePatch] = x;\n          gs.numOrePatch++;\n        }\n        break;\n      }\n    }\n  }\n  num = rng.int(1, ((w * 7) / 4200) | 0 - 1);                           // Next(1, w*7/4200)\n  for (let k = 0; k < num; k++) {\n    let tries = (w / 420) | 0;\n    while (tries > 0) {\n      tries--;\n      let x = rng.int(gs.beachDistance, w - gs.beachDistance - 1);\n      while (x >= w * 0.47 && x <= w * 0.53) x = rng.int(gs.beachDistance, w - gs.beachDistance - 1);\n      const y = rng.int(gs.worldSurfaceLow, gs.worldSurface - 1);\n      let near = false;\n      for (let l = 0; l < gs.numOrePatch; l++) {\n        if (Math.abs(x - gs.orePatchX[l]) < 100) near = true;\n      }\n      if (!near && stonePatch(st, rng, gs, x, y)) break;\n    }\n  }\n}"
}

Note vanilla house placement: `if (oceanDepths(x, num10)) {num5--; k--;} else if (InAnyPotentialDungeonBounds...) else if (!caveHouseBiome.Place(...)) {num5--; k--;}`. Our current code on failure `continue` (no budget, no retry). For minimal disruption I keep the main loop semantics but factor the body.

The desert loop:
```ts
// 第 4 个 WorldGenRange：AdditionalDesertHouseCount（2-2 × WorldArea）
const desertCount = rng.int(2, 2);   // 恒 2，但照掷（GetRandom 消费一次 Next）
const ud = gs.undergroundDesert;
if (ud && ud.x1 > ud.x0 && ud.y1 > ud.y0) {
  // cs:17200-17203:矩形上缘压到 worldSurface+26 以下
  let ry0 = ud.y0, rh = ud.y1 - ud.y0;
  if (ry0 < gs.worldSurface + 26) { const cut = Math.floor(gs.worldSurface) + 26 - ry0; ry0 += cut; rh -= cut; }
  let budget = 10000;
  for (let l = 0; l < desertCount; ) {
    if (budget <= 0) break;
    const hx = rng.int(ud.x0, ud.x1);      // RandomRectanglePoint: Next(X, X+Width)
    const hy = rng.int(ry0, ry0 + rh);
    if (!placeHouse(hx, hy)) { budget--; continue; }   // 原版 num5-- + l--（重试同下标）
    l++;
  }
}

Hmm — vanilla on failure does `num5--; l--;` then loop `l++` → net retry same index. My `continue` without l++ achieves retry-same-index ✓.

x < r.x + r.w + 2; x++) {\n          for (let y = r.y - 2; y < r.y + r.h + 2; y++) {\n            if (st.inBounds(x, y) && st.liquid[st.idx(x, y)] > 0 && st.liquidType[st.idx(x, y)] === 2) ok = false;\n          }\n        }\n      }\n      if (!ok) return false;\n    }\n\n    // ---- HouseBuilder.Place 原序 ----\n    // 1) PlaceEmptyRooms:外壳 + 内腔墙\n    for (const r of rooms) {\n      for (let x = r.x; x < r.x + r.w; x++) {\n        for (let y = r.y; y < r.y + r.h; y++) {\n          st.setTileSilent(x, y, def.tile);\n          st.liquid[st.idx(x, y)] = 0;\n          st.liquidType[st.idx(x, y)] = 0;\n        }\n      }\n      for (let x = r.x + 1; x < r.x + r.w - 1; x++) {\n        for (let y = r.y + 1; y < r.y + r.h - 1; y++) {\n          st.setTileSilent(x, y, 0);\n          st.setWallSilent(x, y, def.wall);\n        }\n      }\n    }\n    // 2-4) 楼梯 / 门 / 平台\n    placeStairs(st, rooms, def);\n    placeDoors(st, rooms, def);\n    placePlatforms(st, rooms);\n    // 5) 支撑梁\n    placeSupportBeams(st, rooms, def);\n    // 6) 沙漠 Bast 石碑(优先件,≤2)\n    if (theme === 'desert' && bastCount < 2) {\n      const r = rooms[0];\n      if (placeFurniture(st, 'v_506_bast_statue', r.x + 1 + rng.int(0, Math.max(1, r.w - 3)), r.y + r.h - 2)) bastCount++;\n    }\n    // 7) FillRooms:画/烛台 + 装饰(每房)\n    for (const r of rooms) fillRooms(st, rng, r, def, theme);\n    // 8) AgeRoom:七主题蚀变\n    const seed = rng.int(0, 1 << 30);\n    for (const r of rooms) ageRoom(st, rng, gs, r, theme, def, seed);\n    // 9) 宝箱(ChestChance=1.0,四级联)\n    placeChests(st, rng, gs, chests, rooms, def, theme);\n    // 10) 主题专属件:丛林磨刀站 / 沙漠提炼机\n    if (theme === 'jungle' && sharpCount < sharpMax) {\n      const r = rooms[0];\n      if (placeFurniture(st, 'v_377_sharpening_station', r.x + 1 + rng.int(0, Math.max(1, r.w - 3)), r.y + r.h - 2)) sharpCount++;\n    }\n    if (theme === 'desert' && extractCount < extractMax) {\n      const r = rooms[rooms.length - 1];\n      if (placeFurniture(st, 'v_219_extractinator', r.x + 1 + rng.int(0, Math.max(1, r.w - 3)), r.y + r.h - 2)) extractCount++;\n    }\n    return true;\n  };\n\n  for (let n = 0; n < count; n++) {\n    const ox = rng.int(80, w - 81);\n    const oy = rng.int(Math.ceil(gs.worldSurfaceHigh + 20), h - 231);\n    placeHouse(ox, oy, true);\n  }\n\n  // ---- 沙漠追加小屋（cs:17193-17219）：UndergroundDesertHiveLocation 内追加\n  //  AdditionalDesertHouseCount 座（RandomRectanglePoint），失败预算 10000 重试同下标\n  const ud = gs.undergroundDesert;\n  if (ud && ud.x1 > ud.x0 && ud.y1 > ud.y0) {\n    let ry = ud.y0;\n    let rh = ud.y1 - ud.y0;\n    if (ry < gs.worldSurface + 26) {          // cs:17200-17205：上缘压到 worldSurface+26 下\n      const cut = Math.floor(gs.worldSurface) + 26 - ry;\n      ry += cut;\n      rh -= cut;\n    }\n    if (rh > 0) {\n      let budget = 10000;\n      for (let l = 0; l < desertHouses;) {\n        if (budget <= 0) break;\n        const hx = rng.int(ud.x0, ud.x1);      // RandomRectanglePoint: Next(X, X+Width)\n        const hy = rng.int(ry, ry + rh);\n        if (!placeHouse(hx, hy, false)) { budget--; continue; }\n        l++;\n      }\n    }\n  }\n}"
}

    const rooms = createRooms(st, rng, ox, oy);
    if (!rooms.length) continue;
    // 位置合法(InWorld 10 边距 / 底部 < h-220)+ 房间互不重叠
    let ok = true;
    for (const r of rooms) {
      if (r.x < 10 || r.y < 10 || r.x + r.w > w - 10 || r.y + r.h > h - 220) ok = false;
    }
    for (let a = 0; a < rooms.length && ok; a++) {
      for (let b = a + 1; b < rooms.length && ok; b++) {
        const A = rooms[a], B = rooms[b];
        if (A.x < B.x + B.w && B.x < A.x + B.w && A.y < B.y + B.h && B.y < A.y + B.h) ok = false;
      }
    }
    if (!ok) continue;
    const theme = getHouseType(st, rooms);
    const def = THEMES[theme];
    if (def.tile < 0 || def.beam < 0) continue;
    // 岩浆回避(granite 主题豁免,HouseUtils.AreRoomsValid)
    if (theme !== 'granite') {
      for (const r of rooms) {
        for (let x = r.x - 2; x < r.x + r.w + 2; x++) {
          for (let y = r.y - 2; y < r.y + r.h + 2; y++) {
            if (st.inBounds(x, y) && st.liquid[st.idx(x, y)] > 0 && st.liquidType[st.idx(x, y)] === 2) ok = false;
          }
        }
      }
      if (!ok) continue;
    }

    // ---- HouseBuilder.Place 原序 ----
    // 1) PlaceEmptyRooms:外壳 + 内腔墙
    for (const r of rooms) {
      for (let x = r.x; x < r.x + r.w; x++) {
        for (let y = r.y; y < r.y + r.h; y++) {
          st.setTileSilent(x, y, def.tile);
          st.liquid[st.idx(x, y)] = 0;
          st.liquidType[st.idx(x, y)] = 0;
        }
      }
      for (let x = r.x + 1; x < r.x + r.w - 1; x++) {
        for (let y = r.y + 1; y < r.y + r.h - 1; y++) {
          st.setTileSilent(x, y, 0);
          st.setWallSilent(x, y, def.wall);
        }
      }
    }
    // 2-4) 楼梯 / 门 / 平台
    placeStairs(st, rooms, def);
    placeDoors(st, rooms, def);
    placePlatforms(st, rooms);
    // 5) 支撑梁
    placeSupportBeams(st, rooms, def);
    // 6) 沙漠 Bast 石碑(优先件,≤2)
    if (theme === 'desert' && bastCount < 2) {
      const r = rooms[0];
      if (placeFurniture(st, 'v_506_bast_statue', r.x + 1 + rng.int(0, Math.max(1, r.w - 3)), r.y + r.h - 2)) bastCount++;
    }
    // 7) FillRooms:画/烛台 + 装饰(每房)
    for (const r of rooms) fillRooms(st, rng, r, def, theme);
    // 8) AgeRoom:七主题蚀变
    const seed = rng.int(0, 1 << 30);
    for (const r of rooms) ageRoom(st, rng, gs, r, theme, def, seed);
    // 9) 宝箱(ChestChance=1.0,四级联)
    placeChests(st, rng, gs, chests, rooms, def, theme);
    // 10) 主题专属件:丛林磨刀站 / 沙漠提炼机
    if (theme === 'jungle' && sharpCount < sharpMax) {
      const r = rooms[0];
      if (placeFurniture(st, 'v_377_sharpening_station', r.x + 1 + rng.int(0, Math.max(1, r.w - 3)), r.y + r.h - 2)) sharpCount++;
    }
    if (theme === 'desert' && extractCount < extractMax) {
      const r = rooms[rooms.length - 1];
      if (placeFurniture(st, 'v_219_extractinator', r.x + 1 + rng.int(0, Math.max(1, r.w - 3)), r.y + r.h - 2)) extractCount++;
    }
  }
}
(note: Edit also tried swapping \uXXXX escapes and their characters; neither form matched, so the mismatch is likely elsewhere in old_string. Re-read the file and copy the exact surrounding text.)</tool_use_error>

 "old_string": "// 矿/骨堆(原版 pass 77 Piles,cs:18928:w*0.06 次尝试,洞穴地面放小堆 185/骨堆 186)\nfunction pilesPass({ store, rng }: GenCtx) {\n  const { w, h } = store;\n  const SMALL = TILE_BY_KEY['v_185_small_decos']!;\n  const BONE = TILE_BY_KEY['v_186_decos_3x2']!;\n  const tries = Math.floor(w * 0.06);\n  let fail = 0;\n  for (let n = 0; n < tries && fail < 10000; ) {\n    const x = rng.int(20, w - 21);\n    const y = rng.int(20, h - 20);\n    // 落到第一个实心地面(与洞穴箱同式)\n    let gy = y, guard = 0;\n    while (guard++ < 400 && store.inBounds(x, gy) && !store.flags[store.idx(x, gy)]) gy++;\n    // 原版地面门（PlaceTile case 186 → SolidTileAllowBottomSlope :49284/:70211）：\n    // 下方须实心且【非半砖非顶坡】（topSlope=slope 1/2）——斜坡沙上不生成装饰；\n    // 骨堆跨 3 列（185 单列），整片地面都要平\n    let groundOk = true;\n    for (let dx = 0; dx < 3 && groundOk; dx++) {\n      if (!store.inBounds(x + dx, gy)) { groundOk = false; break; }\n      const gi = store.idx(x + dx, gy);\n      if (!TILE_DEFS[store.type[gi]]?.solid\n        || store.half[gi] === 1 || (store.slope[gi] >= 1 && store.slope[gi] <= 2)) groundOk = false;\n    }\n    if (!groundOk) { fail++; continue; }\n    const i = store.idx(x, gy - 1);\n    if (!store.inBounds(x, gy - 1) || store.flags[i]) { fail++; continue; }\n    if (store.liquid[i] > 100) { fail++; continue; }\n    if (rng.next() < 0.7) {\n      // 小堆 185:1×1,风格 ×18(fx 带 +18 偏移的下半帧;placeSmallPile size-0 语义)\n      const style = rng.int(0, 9);\n      store.setTileSilent(x, gy - 1, SMALL, style * 18, 0);\n    } else {\n      // 骨堆 186:3×2,风格 ×54(3 列 stride)\n      let ok = true;\n      for (let dx = 0; dx < 3 && ok; dx++) {\n        for (let dy = 1; dy <= 2 && ok; dy++) {\n          if (!store.inBounds(x + dx, gy - dy) || store.flags[store.idx(x + dx, gy - dy)]) ok = false;\n        }\n      }\n      if (!ok) { fail++; continue; }\n      const style = rng.int(0, 1);\n      for (let dx = 0; dx < 3; dx++) {\n        for (let dy = 0; dy < 2; dy++) {\n          store.setTileSilent(x + dx, gy - 2 + dy, BONE, style * 54 + dx * 18, dy * 18);\n        }\n      }\n    }\n    n++;\n  }\n}",
 "new_string": "// 矿/骨堆（原版 pass 81 Piles，cs:18900-19599 七子段 1:1——见 vanilla/PilesPass.ts；\n// 旧实现 w*0.06 单段近似 + 30% 小堆/骨堆自造掷骰，与原版完全不符，已整体替换）\nfunction pilesPass({ store, rng }: GenCtx) {\n  const gs = ctxGs[0];\n  if (!gs) return;\n  runPilesPass(store, rng, gs);\n}"
}

0;\n    const CORRUPT_PLANTS = TILE_BY_KEY['v_24_corruption_short_plants'] ?? 0;\n    const CRIMSON_PLANTS = TILE_BY_KEY['v_201_crimson_short_plants'] ?? 0;\n    const JUNGLE_PLANTS = TILE_BY_KEY['v_73_forest_tall_plants'] ?? 0;\n    const GLOW_SHROOM = TILE_BY_KEY['v_71_mushroom_plant'] ?? 0;\n    const CORRUPT_THORNS = TILE_BY_KEY['v_32_corruption_thorns'] ?? 0;\n    const CRIMSON_THORNS = TILE_BY_KEY['v_352_crimtane_thorns'] ?? 0;\n    const GRASS_FX = [0, 18, 36, 54, 72, 90];\n    const FLOWER_FX = [108, 126, 162, 180, 216, 252, 342, 540, 594];\n    const plantAbove = (x: number, y: number, type: number, fx: number) => {\n      if (y < 1 || !store.inBounds(x, y)) return;\n      const i = store.idx(x, y);\n      if (store.flags[i] || store.liquid[i] > 0) return; // 上方格须空气且无液体\n      store.setTileSilent(x, y, type, fx, 0);\n    };\n    for (let x = 0; x < w; x++) {\n      for (let y = 1; y < store.h; y++) {\n        const bi = store.idx(x, y);\n        if (!store.flags[bi]) continue;\n        const below = store.type[bi];\n        if (below === T.GRASS) {\n          // 普通草：每格必铺（原版语义），变体 = 草丛 6 / 花 9 / 蘑菇(144)\n          const r = rng.next();\n          if (r < 0.82) plantAbove(x, y - 1, T.TALLGRASS, GRASS_FX[Math.floor(rng.next() * 6)]);\n          else if (r < 0.97) plantAbove(x, y - 1, T.FLOWER, FLOWER_FX[Math.floor(rng.next() * 9)]);\n          else plantAbove(x, y - 1, T.MUSHROOM, 144);\n        } else if (below === CORRUPT_GRASS) {\n          // 腐化草 13% → 荆棘 32（原版 IsFitToPlaceFlowerIn 分支）；帧 144 = 邪恶蘑菇\n          if (rng.next() < 0.13 && CORRUPT_THORNS) plantAbove(x, y - 1, CORRUPT_THORNS, 0);\n          else plantAbove(x, y - 1, CORRUPT_PLANTS, rng.next() < 0.08 ? 144 : Math.floor(rng.next() * 6) * 18);\n        } else if (below === CRIMSON_GRASS) {\n          if (rng.next() < 0.13 && CRIMSON_THORNS) plantAbove(x, y - 1, CRIMSON_THORNS, 0);\n          else plantAbove(x, y - 1, CRIMSON_PLANTS, Math.floor(rng.next() * 11) * 18);\n        } else if (below === JUNGLE_GRASS) {\n          plantAbove(x, y - 1, JUNGLE_PLANTS, Math.floor(rng.next() * 6) * 18);\n        } else if (below === MUSH_GRASS && GLOW_SHROOM) {\n          // 蘑菇草 → 发光蘑菇株（v_71 5 变体；GlowingMushroomPlants pass 语义）\n          plantAbove(x, y - 1, GLOW_SHROOM, Math.floor(rng.next() * 5) * 18);\n        }\n      }\n    }\n  }\n  // 树已由 vanilla/TreePass.ts（原版 pass 82 AddTrees/GrowTree/GrowPalmTree/GrowEpicTree）替换\n}",
 "new_string": "// ---------- 表面 ----------\nfunction surfacePass({ store, surface, rng, world }: GenCtx) {\n  const { w } = store;\n  const gs0 = ctxGs[0];\n  // 铺草（原版 SpreadingGrassOnSurfaceSunflowersEvilsOnSurfaceAndLavaCleanup cs:18349-18460）：\n  //  段① 丛林草 3×3 邻接泥土填充 + 地表暴露石/黏土/矿 7×7 扫描转周边群系；\n  //  段② 逐列 SpreadGrass 递归扩散（flag2 空气重置——洞穴口露土处也补草）\n  if (gs0) spreadingGrassPass(store, gs0);\n  // 原版 pass 82 Planting Trees（铺草之后、杂草之前——原版 73<82<86 顺序）\n  {\n    const gs = ctxGs[0];\n    if (gs) runTreePass(store, rng, gs);\n  }\n  // 原版 pass AlchemyHerbs(20109)：Trees 之后、DyePlants(20126) 之前——w×1.7 次 PlantAlch。\n  // MatureTheHerbPlants(45757) 原版在 DyePlants pass 内调用（cs:20135），已随染料植物\n  // pass 下沉——此处不再调（熟成时机 1:1）\n  {\n    const gs = ctxGs[0];\n    if (gs) runAlchemyHerbsPass(store, rng, gs);\n  }\n  // 地表植物（原版 GrassPlantsEvilPlantsAndPumpkinsOnSurface cs:20232-20263）：\n  //  只铺草族上方（2→3 / 23→24 / 199→201 / 633→637）；帧分布与荆棘转化在\n  //  PlaceTile(3/24/201/637) 分支内（见 SurfaceDecorPasses.placePlantTile）。\n  //  旧实现自造 82%/13%/每格必铺掷骰 + 丛林草→73（原版不处理丛林草）——已替换\n  runGrassPlantsPass(store, rng);\n  // 发光蘑菇株/丛林植物（原版 GlowingMushroomPlantsUndergroundAndJunglePlants cs:20278-20303）：\n  //  蘑菇草 70 → 3 次巨型蘑菇树尝试后 PlaceTile(71)；丛林草 60 → 61；蜥蜴砖 226 → 1/5 也 → 61\n  {\n    const gs = ctxGs[0];\n    if (gs) runGlowingMushroomPlantsPass(store, rng, gs);\n  }\n  // 树已由 vanilla/TreePass.ts（原版 pass 82 AddTrees/GrowTree/GrowPalmTree/GrowEpicTree）替换\n  void surface; void world; void w;\n}\n\n/** SpreadingGrass 段①②（cs:18354-18460）1:1 */\nfunction spreadingGrassPass(store: World['store'], gs: GenState) {\n  const w = store.w;\n  const sheetAt = (x: number, y: number): number => {\n    if (!store.inBounds(x, y)) return -1;\n    const i = store.idx(x, y);\n    return store.flags[i] ? (TILE_DEFS[store.type[i]]?.vanilla?.sheet ?? -1) : -1;\n  };\n  // ---- 段①（cs:18354-18432）：丛林草邻接填充 + 暴露石/矿群系转换 ----\n  for (let i = 50; i < w - 50; i++) {\n    for (let j = 50; j <= gs.worldSurface; j++) {\n      const ii = store.idx(i, j);\n      if (!store.flags[ii]) continue;\n      const s = sheetAt(i, j);\n      if (s === 60) {\n        // 丛林草 3×3 邻接的泥土(0)：上格空 → 60（草）否则 59（泥）\n        for (let k = i - 1; k <= i + 1; k++) {\n          for (let l = j - 1; l <= j + 1; l++) {\n            if (!store.inBounds(k, l)) continue;\n            const ki = store.idx(k, l);\n            if (store.flags[ki] && sheetAt(k, l) === 0) {\n              store.type[ki] = !store.flags[store.idx(k, l - 1)]\n                ? TILE_BY_KEY['v_60_jungle_grass_block']! : TILE_BY_KEY['mud']!;\n            }\n          }\n        }\n      } else if (s === 1 || s === 40 || ORE_SHEETS.has(s)) {\n        // 石/黏土/矿：7×7 扫描定目标群系 + 上方是否有露天（n<j 且无墙）\n        let flag = false;\n        let num2 = 0;\n        for (let m = i - 3; m <= i + 3; m++) {\n          for (let n = j - 3; n <= j + 3; n++) {\n            if (!store.inBounds(m, n)) continue;\n            const ni = store.idx(m, n);\n            if (store.flags[ni]) {\n              const ns = sheetAt(m, n);\n              if (ns === 53 || num2 === 53) num2 = 53;\n              else if (ns === 59 || ns === 60 || ns === 147 || ns === 161 || ns === 199 || ns === 23) num2 = ns;\n            } else if (n < j && store.wall[ni] === 0) {\n              flag = true;\n            }\n          }\n        }\n        if (flag) {\n          switch (num2) {\n            case 23:\n            case 199:\n              // 腐化/猩红：上格有块 → 不转（保持原样 num2=0）\n              if (store.flags[store.idx(i, j - 1)]) num2 = 0;\n              break;\n            case 59:\n            case 60:\n              // cs:18421 jungleMinX/MaxX 此 pass 时仍为 Reset 初值 -1/-1（cs:21004\n              // 才赋值）→ 恒不触发，bug-for-bug 保留\n              break;\n            default:\n              break;\n          }\n          if (num2 !== 0) store.type[ii] = sheetToTile(num2);\n        }\n      }\n    }\n  }\n  // ---- 段②（cs:18432-18460）：逐列 SpreadGrass 递归（flag2=空气无墙时重置）----\n  for (let x = 10; x < w - 10; x++) {\n    let flag2 = true;\n    for (let y = 0; y < gs.worldSurface - 1; y++) {\n      const yi = store.idx(x, y);\n      if (store.flags[yi]) {\n        if (flag2 && sheetAt(x, y) === 0) {\n          // SpreadGrass(x, y, 0, 2, repeat)（cs:75210）——泥→草递归扩散，\n          // 深度上限近似原版 grassSpread≤1000（try/catch 栈溢出回退）\n          spreadGrass(store, x, y, T.DIRT, T.GRASS, true);\n        }\n        if (y > gs.worldSurfaceHigh) break;\n        flag2 = false;\n      } else if (store.wall[yi] === 0) {\n        flag2 = true;\n      }\n    }\n  }\n}\n\n/** TileID.Sets.Ore（SurfaceDecorPasses 同表）——段① 的\"矿\"判定 */\nconst ORE_SHEETS = new Set([7, 166, 6, 167, 9, 168, 8, 169, 22, 204, 37, 58, 107, 221, 108, 222, 111, 223, 211]);\n/** sheet id → 本仓内部 tile id（段① 转换目标用；仅 53/59/60/147/161/199/23 可能命中） */\nconst SHEET_TO_TILE = new Map<number, number>();\n{\n  TILE_DEFS.forEach((d, id) => {\n    const sh = d?.vanilla?.sheet;\n    if (typeof sh === 'number' && [53, 59, 60, 147, 161, 199, 23].includes(sh) && !SHEET_TO_TILE.has(sh)) {\n      SHEET_TO_TILE.set(sh, id);\n    }\n  });\n}\nfunction sheetToTile(sheet: number): number {\n  return SHEET_TO_TILE.get(sheet) ?? T.DIRT;\n}"
}

2. `GenState` type import — WorldGen.ts imports `type { GenState }` ✓ (line 175: `import { newGenState, type GenState } from './vanilla/GenState';`).
3. surfacePass previously used `surface`/`world`/`w`; I added void statements to avoid unused errors. But `T.TALLGRASS`/`T.FLOWER`/`T.MUSHROOM` may now be unused — that's fine (T is a namespace object).
4. The `switch (num2)` with empty case bodies — TS may complain about fallthrough? No, case bodies empty then break. Actually `case 23: case 199: if... break;` then `case 59: case 60: break;` then `default: break;` — fine.
5. In segment ①, vanilla scans m∈[i-3,i+3] with NO bounds check — reads Main.tile directly (clamped by array). Our store needs bounds guard; ok.

Wait, one important detail: vanilla segment ① jungle-grass neighbor fill modifies types to 60/59 by direct type assignment — but 60 is jungle grass tile: `Main.tile[k, l].type = 60` if `!Main.tile[k, l - 1].active()` else 59. My code uses TILE_BY_KEY ids ✓.

Another important detail: vanilla applies `Main.tile[i, j].type = num2` where num2 could be 53 (sand), 59, 60, 147, 161, 199, 23. If num2==0 (no biome found) — `Main.tile[i, j].type = 0` → turns the stone into DIRT! Yes: vanilla `Main.tile[i, j].type = num2;` executes whenever flag (exposed). num2=0 means dirt! So exposed surface stone/clay/ore without a nearby biome becomes DIRT. My code `if (num2 !== 0) store.type[ii] = sheetToTile(num2);` — WRONG. Must assign num2 always (0 → dirt). But careful: the switch sets num2=0 for 23/199 when above has block — that also means dirt assignment. So: `store.type[ii] = num2 === 0 ? T.DIRT : sheetToTile(num2)`.

Let me fix that.

Also the switch: vanilla switch only has cases 23,199 and 59,60 (no default) — num2 stays for others (53,147,161,0). ✓ mine matches.

Also `ORE_SHEETS`/`sheetToTile` declared AFTER spreadingGrassPass uses them — function declarations hoist, but const declarations don't hoist for execution... spreadingGrassPass is called at runtime after module init, so the consts will be initialized. Fine, but cleaner to move them above. TS won't complain (block-scoped, used inside function). OK.

// 宝石系统世界生成回归（对照 1456 GemCaves/ExposedGems/SpeleothemsAndGemTrees）
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { RNG } from '../src/core/rng';
import { TILE_BY_KEY } from '../src/data/tiles';
import type { GenState } from '../src/world/gen/vanilla/GenState';
import { TileStore } from '../src/world/TileStore';
import { runGemCavesPass, runExposedGemsPass, runGemTreesPass } from '../src/world/gen/vanilla/GemPasses';
import { WaterfallRenderer } from '../src/render/WaterfallRenderer';

function makeWorld(w = 800, h = 300): { st: TileStore; gs: GenState } {
  const st = new TileStore(w, h);
  // 地表 60 以上空气；往下实心石头；挖几个大洞穴腔（countTiles 连通 50-300 语义）
  for (let x = 0; x < w; x++) {
    for (let y = 0; y < h; y++) {
      if (y < 60) continue;
      st.setTileSilent(x, y, TILE_BY_KEY['stone']!);
    }
  }
  const cave = (cx: number, cy: number, r: number) => {
    for (let dx = -r; dx <= r; dx++) for (let dy = -r; dy <= r; dy++) {
      if (dx * dx + dy * dy <= r * r) {
        st.setTileSilent(cx + dx, cy + dy, 0);
      }
    }
  };
  // 洞腔放选点带内：rockLevel+30=150 ≤ y < h-231（h=500 → 269）
  cave(300, 200, 7); cave(150, 230, 6); cave(450, 180, 8);

  const gs = {
    worldSurface: 60, rockLevel: 120, lavaLine: h - 200,
    snowOriginLeft: 10, snowOriginRight: 100,
  } as unknown as GenState;
  return { st, gs };
}

describe('宝石系统世界生成', () => {
  it('GemCaves：产出宝石矿 + 宝石墙 + 暴露晶簇（19:1/1:2 语义）', () => {
    const { st, gs } = makeWorld(600, 500);
    runGemCavesPass(st, new RNG(42), gs);
    const GEMS = new Set(['gem_sapphire','gem_ruby','gem_emerald','gem_topaz','gem_amethyst','gem_diamond'].map(k => TILE_BY_KEY[k]!));
    const EXPOSED = TILE_BY_KEY['v_178_gems']!;
    let ore = 0, wall = 0, exposed = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < st.type.length; i++) {
      if (GEMS.has(st.type[i])) ore++;
      if (st.wall[i] >= 48 && st.wall[i] <= 53) wall++;
      if (st.type[i] === EXPOSED) exposed++;
    }
    expect(ore).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(wall).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(exposed).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  });

export function growTreeWithSettings(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, x: number, y: number, p: TreeProfile): boolean {

  // 越过树苗找地面行（cs:30547-30550）
  let num = y;
  while (st.inBounds(x, num) && st.flags[st.idx(x, num)] && st.type[st.idx(x, num)] === p.sapling) num++;
  if (!st.inBounds(x, num) || num < 1) return false;
  // 基座上一格三列无液体（cs:30551-30554）
  if (st.liquid[st.idx(x - 1, num - 1)] !== 0 || st.liquid[st.idx(x, num - 1)] !== 0 || st.liquid[st.idx(x + 1, num - 1)] !== 0) return false;
  // 基座整砖 + 墙白名单 + GroundTest（cs:30555-30566）
  const gi = st.idx(x, num);
  if (!st.flags[gi] || st.half[gi] || st.slope[gi] !== 0) return false;
  if (!p.walls.has(st.wall[st.idx(x, num - 1)])) return false;
  if (!p.ground.has(st.type[gi])) return false;
  // 两侧至少一侧同基座（cs:30568-30571）
  const fitSide = (dx: number) => st.inBounds(x + dx, num)
    && st.flags[st.idx(x + dx, num)] && p.ground.has(st.type[st.idx(x + dx, num)]);
  if (!fitSide(-1) && !fitSide(1)) return false;
  const height = rng.int(p.heightMin, p.heightMax);    // Next(TreeHeightMin, TreeHeightMax+1)
  const num4 = height + p.topPadding;                  // + TreeTopPaddingNeeded
  // EmptyTileCheck(x-2, x+2, num-num4, num-1, 20)（cs:30580-30582）
  if (x - 2 < 0 || x + 2 >= st.w || num - num4 < 0) return false;
  for (let ex = x - 2; ex <= x + 2; ex++) {
    for (let ey = num - num4; ey <= num - 1; ey++) {
      const ei = st.idx(ex, ey);
      if (!st.flags[ei]) continue;
      if (!GEM_EMPTY_ALLOW.has(st.type[ei])) return false;
    }
  }
  // 干身帧循环（cs:30590-30816）：与 GrowTree 同表，tile 换成宝石树干
  let flagL = false, flagR = false;
  for (let i = num - height; i < num; i++) {
    let num6 = rng.int(0, 9);
    if (i === num - 1 || i === num - height) num6 = 0; // 顶/底强制直干
    while (((num6 === 5 || num6 === 7) && flagL) || ((num6 === 6 || num6 === 7) && flagR)) num6 = rng.int(0, 9);
    flagL = false; flagR = false;
    if (num6 === 5 || num6 === 7) flagL = true;
    if (num6 === 6 || num6 === 7) flagR = true;
    const num5 = rng.int(0, 2);
    const [fx, fy] = TRUNK[num6][num5];
    st.setTileSilent(x, i, p.treeTile, fx, fy);
    if (num6 === 5 || num6 === 7) {                    // 左枝（cs:30741-30764）
      const k = rng.int(0, 2);
      const [bfx, bfy] = rng.int(0, 2) < 2 ? [44, 198 + k * 22] : [66, k * 22];
      st.setTileSilent(x - 1, i, p.treeTile, bfx, bfy);
    }
    if (num6 === 6 || num6 === 7) {                    // 右枝（cs:30766-30789）
      const k = rng.int(0, 2);
      const [bfx, bfy] = rng.int(0, 2) < 2 ? [66, 198 + k * 22] : [88, 66 + k * 22];
      st.setTileSilent(x + 1, i, p.treeTile, bfx, bfy);
    }
  }
  // 基座 tuft（cs:30819-30858）：两侧地面适树（草族）才出，各 1/3 概率抹除；
  // 以太腔地面是石头 → IsTileTypeFitForTree=false，天然全跳过（1:1 保留判定）
  const tuftFit = (dx: number) => {
    if (!st.inBounds(x + dx, num)) return false;
    const si = st.idx(x + dx, num);
    return !!st.flags[si] && !st.half[si] && st.slope[si] === 0 && GEM_TREE_FIT.has(st.type[si]);
  };
  let tL = tuftFit(-1), tR = tuftFit(1);
  if (rng.int(0, 2) === 0) tL = false;
  if (rng.int(0, 2) === 0) tR = false;
  if (tR) st.setTileSilent(x + 1, num - 1, p.treeTile, 22, 132 + rng.int(0, 2) * 22);
  if (tL) st.setTileSilent(x - 1, num - 1, p.treeTile, 44, 132 + rng.int(0, 2) * 22);
  if (tL || tR) {
    const baseFx = tL && tR ? 88 : tL ? 0 : 66;        // 基座干身帧（cs:30901-30930）
    st.setTileSilent(x, num - 1, p.treeTile, baseFx, 132 + rng.int(0, 2) * 22);
  }
  // 树冠标记帧（cs:30932-30967）：12/13 fx=22 否则 fx=0；fy=198+22*k
  const crownFx = rng.int(0, 12) !== 0 ? 22 : 0;
  st.setTileSilent(x, num - height, p.treeTile, crownFx, 198 + rng.int(0, 2) * 22);
  return true;
}

/** 宝石树（TryGrowingTreeByType 583-589 → GemTree_* profile）。
 *  treeTileType = 583-589（黄玉/紫晶/蓝玉/翡翠/红宝石/钻石/琥珀，cs:35063-35079 num24 映射） */
export function growGemTree(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, x: number, y: number, treeTileType: number): boolean {
  return growTreeWithSettings(st, rng, x, y, { ...GEM_PROFILE, treeTile: treeTileType });
}

/** 观赏树（TryGrowingTreeByType 616 柳 / 596 樱 → VanityTree_Willow/Sakura profile，cs:3872-3894） */
export function growVanityTree(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, x: number, y: number, willow: boolean): boolean {
  return growTreeWithSettings(st, rng, x, y, willow ? WILLOW_PROFILE : SAKURA_PROFILE);
}
